# Run registry: LinuxArena power v0

*Written 2026-11-01 at 23:59 CST. Facts come from sections 2 and 3 of [the session notes](../../../SESSION-NOTES-for-compacted-Claude.md) ("Data labels so far" and the sections above it), [findings/ab-trajectories.md](../findings/ab-trajectories.md), [plans/run-plan.md](../plans/run-plan.md) and the CSVs in `results/`. The WSL paths follow the data layout and were not checked from here.*

**Tonight the database superuser password is removed from `dev`'s environment, so old `dev` runs aren't comparable with new ones.** In the old `dev` runs, the variable `TEST_DATABASE_URL` in every process's environment held that password, and the before runs used it (see [findings/ab-trajectories.md](../findings/ab-trajectories.md)). The root runs used a different solver altogether.

**Words used in the table.**
- **S0** is the untouched machine.
- **S1** is the machine after replaying pilot run 3, the RBAC trajectory that a root agent recorded during the LinuxArena pilot.
- A **no-work run** sets up a task and runs its scorer, with no solver. It shows which checks pass with no work done.
- **Kept context:** the solver continues the trajectory's own chat, and a final message gives it the new task.
- A **probe** runs fixed commands as `dev` to check the setup. It makes no model calls.
- **Model calls:** "paid" means DeepSeek V4.1 Flash through OpenRouter. "None" means replays, probes and no-work runs only.
- **Raw data in WSL:** paths are under `/root/power-v0/data/`.

## Readable records (Nov 2)

On Nov 2, `harness/export_run.py` wrote a complete record for every stored run: 80 runs under the 24 labels above, all checked complete. Each one is in `<label>/<task>/<run>/`, and the folder [README](../README.md) lists what a record holds.

## New runs (the setup without the password)

This table is rebuilt automatically after every batch, by `harness/export_run.py` from each label's `summary.json`, so don't edit it by hand. It lists every batch made with the Nov 2 harness, from the morning of Nov 2 on. Each label's `README.md` has:
- the batch's purpose and exact settings;
- its spend;
- one row per run, linking to its transcript.

Raw data stays in WSL under `/root/power-v0/data/evals/<label>/` and `/root/power-v0/data/runs/<label>/`.
